## 23. Architecture of `devices/Transient Split.amxd` + `devices/Transient Split.adg` (slug `transplit`)

Bitwig "Transient Split (Spectral)" look and parameters. Channel section (per-channel Gain / Pan / On / Solo / FX chain) = the Audio Effect Rack chain's own volume, pan, activator, solo and devices; Pre FX / Post FX = devices before / after the rack. Inspector parameters are ordinary parameters on the face.

### Presentation Layout (`440 x 169` px)
- **One `v8ui` canvas** (`obj-viz`, `x = 6, y = 6, w = 284, h = 112`, `src/transplit_ui.js`, the only v8ui; hosts `spectral_link`), laid out like Bitwig's display:
  - Top row: `Tilt 0.00 %` and `Decay 8.00` (yellow values; drag vertically to edit, double-click resets).
  - Left vertical slider (`x = 5..17`): **Analysis Bias** (-100..+100 %, orange centre marker, drag / double-click resets). Right vertical slider (`x = 267..279`): **Transients/Tones Blend** (0..100 %, centre marker at 50 %).
  - Plot (`x = 22..262, y = 18..96`): **Display Style Waveform** = scrolling amplitude view (80 telemetry frames, about 1.7 s), Transients above the zero line in yellow, Tones below in blue (-60..0 dB scale); **Sonogram** = 60 x 22 frequency-domain history, cells coloured blue (tone) to yellow (transient). `PRE | POST` toggle (bottom row): Pre shows the analysis split, Post the channel output after Tilt / Blend (the channel that is not this instance's output is dimmed). Resolved channel pill (`OUT 1 TRANSIENTS`) and chain chip (`CH 1/2 LINKED`, `NO RACK`) sit in the plot corners.
  - Bottom row: the two Display Style icons (burst = Waveform, bars = Sonogram, orange when selected), `PRE | POST`, `Smoothing 1.50` (blue, draggable). Style and Pre / Post are the hidden `live.text` parameters `Display Style` (`Waveform` / `Sonogram`) and `Display Post`, `parameter_invisible 1`, not linked.
- **Right column** (`x = 300..432`, rows `9 + 19 i`): `Tilt`, `Decay`, `Smoothing`, `Bias`, `Blend`, `Mix` value bars (the real automatable parameters; the canvas edits drive them through `route`). `Latency 4096 smp` below.
- **Under the canvas**: `Type` tab (`Percussive | Noise`), `Tilt Mode` tab (`Standard | Contour`), `Size` tab (`512 | 1k | 2k | 4k`, default `1k`, extra), `Output` menu (`Auto | Transients | Tones | All`).
- No `live.*` widget overlaps the v8ui, the two background panels are the last boxes.

### Parameters (Live short names are unique)
| Name | Range | Default | Gen message |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Tilt` (Tilt Amount) | -100..+100 % | 0 | `tiltamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Decay` (Transients Decay) | 1.00..125.00 blocks, float, unit `%.2f` | 8.00 | `tdecay` |
| `Smoothing` (Tones Smoothing) | 1.00..64.00 blocks, float | 1.50 | `tsmooth` |
| `Bias` (Analysis Bias) | -100..+100 % | 0 | `anbias` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Blend` (Transients Tones Blend) | 0..100 % | 50 | `blendamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Mix` | 0..100 % | 100 | `mixamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Type` | Percussive / Noise | Percussive | `ttype` |
| `Tilt Mode` | Standard / Contour | Standard | `tmode` |
| `Size` | 512 / 1k / 2k / 4k | 1k | `fftn` (`512 * 2^i`) |
| `Output` | Auto / Transients / Tones / All | Auto | face -> `outsel 0|1|2` |

Linked keys (shared by every instance in the rack): all except `Output` and the display toggles (`ttype bias tdecay tsmooth tilt tmode blend mix size`). Renamed from the previous version: `T Decay` -> `Decay`, `T Smooth` -> `Smoothing` (long names unchanged); `Blend` was -100..+100 cross-feed, now 0..100 Mix-style balance; `Decay` / `Smoothing` were Int 0..16 blocks, now float; `Mix` is new; `Display Style` values `Spectrum` -> `Waveform`. Do not name a Param or function argument `noise`, `rise`, `fall`, `bias`, `mix`... (GenExpr operators): the core uses `nzMode`, `rsK`, `flK`, `anbias`, `tiltamt`, `blendamt`, `mixamt`.

### DSP (`src/transplit_core.genexpr`; JS reference `tsMasks()` in `src/transplit_config.js`)
Mask-STFT of `spectral_split_lib` (hop N/4, Mid analysis, one real mask applied to L and R). Per hop and bin (`ts_masks`):
- `A[k]` = rms of the Mid amplitude over +-2 bins. **Percussive**: onset = `20 log10(A / H)` through a smoothstep between `d0 = 4.5 - 12 * Bias` and `d0 + 5.5` dB; `H` is a history of `A` (rise 14 ms, fall 40 ms). **Noise**: phase instability = `|phi(t) - 2 phi(t-1) + phi(t-2)| / pi`, (1, 2, 1) smoothed across bins, smoothstep between `0.10 - 0.12 Bias` and `0.30 - 0.27 Bias`.
- `Decay` (blocks = hops, fractional): `tx = max(ev, tx_prev * exp(-1 / Decay))`. `Smoothing` (fractional): `tone = max(1 - tx, qx_prev * exp(-1 / Smoothing) * A_prev / A)`. `mask(Tones) = tone`, `mask(Transients) = 1 - tone`: exact sum 1.
- `Tilt` (explicit gain): Transients `* g`, Tones `/ g`, `g = 10^(12 dB * Tilt * w(f) / 20)`; `w` = -1 at 100 Hz .. +1 at 10 kHz (Standard) or **-1 around 1 kHz and +1 at the lows and highs (Contour: a positive amount raises highs and lows together)**.
- `Blend` b = 0..1: Transients mask `* min(1, 2 b)`, Tones mask `* min(1, 2 (1 - b))`. 50 % = both 1 = the original is reconstructed; 100 % only the transient portions (Tones silent, Transients unchanged); 0 % only the tones.
- **Mix**: `out = mix * wet + (1 - mix) * dry * kShare`, `kShare = 0.5` when the instance outputs one channel of the rack (the 2 chains sum to `mix * wet + (1 - mix) * dry`), `1` for Output All (outside a rack: plain dry/wet). Dry goes through its own 4096-sample delay; `mix` is one-pole smoothed (no clicks), the Size-change mute only affects the wet. Caveat: the dry share passes through each chain's devices.
- State is reset (`age = 0`) on Size changes and when the device wakes from silence. Silence sleep: input below `1e-5` for a whole frame skips all FFT work. A Size change mutes the wet for `4096 + N + 128` samples.
- Constant latency 4096 (`Delay padL / padR` for the wet, `dryL / dryR` for the dry, patcher `"latency": 4096`).

### Telemetry (`buffer~ ---transplitscope @samps 800`, bound on loadbang by `scope ---transplitscope`; the face gets `setbuffer ---transplitscope`)
`0..127` Mid level per column, `128..255` Transients post, `256..383` Tones post (0..1 over -100..0 dB), `384..511` transient fraction of the column, `512` frame counter (wraps at 102400, a multiple of 64), `513` samplerate, `514` N, `515` awake, `516` input peak, `517` mean Transients mask, `518` latency, `519` output selection. **`530..785` waveform ring**: frame `f` at slot `f mod 64`, 4 floats (rms of the Transients and Tones signal pre = analysis masks, then post = final masks; `ts_wave`). Written about every 1024 samples; the face polls at 15 fps, reads every new ring entry since its last poll and only redraws when the frame counter moves. The Sonogram's yellow / blue fraction comes from the post masks (identical to pre at Tilt 0 / Blend 50 %).

### Rack preset
`devices/Transient Split.adg`: one Audio Effect Rack, chains `Transients` and `Tones`, each holding the device with `Output = Auto`.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_transplit.py && node tests/test_transplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Transient Split.amxd"   # preview: previews/transient_split.png
scripts/vm_spectral.sh audio transplit        # real Max, in the VM (optional case names)
```
`tests/test_transplit.js`: reference sum identity at Blend 50 % (< -250 dB at every Size, both types), onset / tone behaviour, Bias monotonic, Tilt / Blend as gains, fractional Decay, `ts_wave`, GenExpr functions transpiled and compared with the reference, lint, link module, built device (parameter table, wiring, `.adg`).
Audio cases (`scripts/run_transplit_audio_tests.py`, three real core instances per case; stimulus: noise bursts over a 440 Hz sine): `sum_*` (Transients + Tones null vs the input delayed 4096), `drums_*`, `bias_*`, `noise_*`, `blend_hi` / `blend_lo`, `mix_50` (T + Q = 0.5 wet + 0.5 dry) and `mix_0` (= dry delayed), `silence`, `latency_*`, telemetry and waveform ring.

### Known rough edges
- Linking is verified with mocks and (per the suite test) in Live; between a parameter change and its application by the peers (80-160 ms) the chains do not sum exactly.
- A rack with more than 2 chains: chains >= 3 resolve to Tones. Instances that are not the first device of their chain still link by rack.
- Percussive onsets are smeared by the Hann window (about N/2 either side). Tones Smoothing is an energy-aware tone extension, not Bitwig-identical (their algorithm is not public). Decay / Smoothing are in this device's own blocks (hops of N/4), so their numbers do not map one-to-one to Bitwig's.
- `scripts/render_preview.py` shows the `live.numbox` units with one decimal (`8.0`); Live shows the `%.2f` unit text.
- Mix: the dry share runs through each chain's devices (Bitwig mixes after the chains).
- CPU is not measured (all work of a hop runs in the sample that completes it; Size 4k is the worst case).
- GenExpr lessons: a user function call nested in the arguments of `poke()` makes the whole codebox compile to silence; an LCG on a `History` with `2^32` literals produced `inf` (use `noise()`).

---

## 24. Architecture of `devices/Loud Split.amxd` + `devices/Loud Split.adg` (slug `loudsplit`)

Spectral Split suite member (framework: section 22). Three channels, chain order 1 Quiet (green), 2 Mid (yellow), 3 Loud (red). Per bin of the Mid spectrum, level above the Higher Threshold goes to Loud, below the Lower Threshold to Quiet, between to Mid; the three masks sum to exactly 1 for every setting (only Tilt weights the analysis, it never changes the output gain), so the chains sum back to the input delayed by 4096.

### Presentation Layout (`480 x 169` px, Bitwig Loud Split (Spectral) layout)
- **One `v8ui` canvas** (`obj-viz`, `x = 6, y = 6, w = 290, h = 157`, `src/loudsplit_ui.js`, the only v8ui; hosts `spectral_link`, 4 outlets). Like Bitwig's display, the four corner values live in the canvas: top-left the `Relative Loudness Mode` toggle (checkbox + label), top-right `Rise 2.00` (orange), bottom-left `Tilt 0.0 dB/oct` (purple), bottom-right `Fall 8.00`. Click the toggle; drag a value vertically (Rise / Fall logarithmic over 0.13..125, Tilt linear over +-6, 150 px for the full range), double-click resets (2 / 8 / 0). They are real, automatable parameters (`Relative`, `Rise`, `Fall`, `Tilt`) whose `live.*` objects are kept off the face (`presentation 0`); canvas edits go outlet 0 -> `route` -> the object, which drives gen and the face as usual.
  - Second HUD row (`y = 18..31`): resolved channel pill (`OUT 1 QUIET` / `OUT 2 MID` / `OUT 3 LOUD` / `OUT ALL`, `SOLO ...` while a dot is held), chain chip, `PRE | POST` (Bitwig's Spectrum Display Pre / Post, default Pre) and `SPEC | SONO` toggles (hidden `live.text` parameters `Display Post` / `Display Style`, not linked).
  - Plot (`x = 4..266, y = 33..141`, log frequency 30 Hz..20 kHz, 128 columns, level axis -110..0 dB): PRE = Mid spectrum bars coloured by the column's Quiet (green) / Mid (yellow) / Loud (red) share, blended where a bin is split; POST = the three channels after the masks; SONO = waterfall in the same colours.
  - **Threshold lines** (Higher red, Lower green; they follow Tilt because the weighted level is what is classified; in Relative mode they ride on the loudest bin of the frame): drag vertically = threshold (Absolute or Relative parameter depending on `Relative`; Lower can't pass Higher), **Alt / Shift / Ctrl-drag = Knee**, double-click = default. The dot handle at the right end (`x = 278`) is the **momentary band solo** (Higher: Loud, Lower: Quiet, while the mouse is held; via `outsel`, not a stored parameter).
- **Right column** (`x = 300..474`), Bitwig's threshold / knee strips: `Higher` + `Hi Knee` (red), `Lower` + `Lo Knee` (green), `Rel Higher` + `Rel Lower` (a greyed pair follows the `Relative` mode via `act_abs` / `act_rel`), then `Size` tab (`1k | 2k | 4k`), `Output` menu (`Auto | Quiet | Mid | Loud | All`), `Mix` value bar and the legend. No `live.*` widget overlaps the v8ui; the two background panels are the last boxes.
- **Bitwig mapping**: channel section (Gain, Pan, On, Solo, FX chain) = the Rack chain's own volume / pan / activator / solo / devices; Pre FX / Post FX = devices before / after the rack; the Output section Mix = the `Mix` parameter below.

### Parameters (name, range, default)
`Higher Threshold` (short `Higher`) -110..0 dB, -30; `Lower Threshold` (`Lower`) -110..0 dB, -85; `Relative Higher Threshold` (`Rel Higher`) -110..0 dB below the loudest bin, -12; `Relative Lower Threshold` (`Rel Lower`) -110..0, -36 (our own defaults: in Relative mode 0 dB is the loudest bin of the block, so Loud = within 12 dB of it, Quiet = more than 36 dB below it); `Higher Knee` / `Lower Knee` 1.0..24.0 dB, 6.0; `Rise Time` 0.13..125.00 blocks (float), 2.00; `Fall Time` 0.13..125.00, 8.00; `Tilt` -6..+6 dB/oct about 1 kHz (analysis only), 0; `Relative Loudness Mode` (`Relative`) Absolute / Relative, Absolute; `Mix` 0..100 %, 100; `Size` 1k / 2k / 4k, 2k; `Output` Auto / Quiet / Mid / Loud / All. Linked across instances: everything except `Output` and the display toggles (`Mix` included). Renamed / removed vs the earlier version: ranges and defaults changed for every threshold / knee / Rise / Fall (was -90..0 / -24 / -48, knee 0..30, Rise / Fall Int 0..32 default 0); `Relative` long name `Relative Loudness` -> `Relative Loudness Mode`; short names are unchanged; new: `Mix`.

### Gen messages
`Higher` -> `thrhi`, `Lower` -> `thrlo`, `Rel Higher` -> `rthrhi`, `Rel Lower` -> `rthrlo`, `Hi Knee` -> `kneehi`, `Lo Knee` -> `kneelo`, `Relative` -> `relmode`, `Rise` -> `risebl`, `Fall` -> `fallbl`, `Tilt` -> `tiltdb`, `Mix` -> `wetamt` (`Mix / 100`; a Param called `mix` would shadow the builtin), `Size` -> `fftn`. `Output` is resolved by the face (`outsel 0|1|2|3`).

### Mix
Each instance outputs `mix * wet_own + (1 - mix) * dry / K` with dry delayed by the same 4096 samples (`Delay dryL/dryR`), K = 3 when one chain of a rack (outsel 1..3), K = 1 for Output All / outside a rack, so the rack sum is `mix * wet + (1 - mix) * dry`. `mix` is smoothed (one-pole, 500 samples) and an exact 1.0 is bit-identical to the previous output. Caveat: the dry share goes through each chain's devices (an FX in the Loud chain also processes dry / 3).

### DSP (`src/loudsplit_core.genexpr`; JS reference `lsMasks()` in `src/loudsplit_config.js`)
Mask-STFT of `spectral_split_lib` (hop N/4, Mid analysis, one real mask applied to L and R). Per hop and bin (`ls_masks`):
- `lv = 20 log10(max(A, 1e-6)) + Tilt * log2(f / 1 kHz)` (A = Mid amplitude, a full-scale sine reads its amplitude; note Mid = (L+R)/2). `Relative`: `lev = lv - max_k lv` (loudest bin 0 dB, bins 1..N/2-1), else `lev = lv`.
- Rise / Fall: per-bin one-pole smoothing of `lev` in dB, coefficient `1 - exp(-1 / Rise)` while `lev` is above the state, `1 - exp(-1 / Fall)` below, fractional blocks clamped to 0.13..125 (0.13 is about instant, blocks = hops of N / 4 samples). The state restarts (`age = 0`) on Size changes and when the device wakes from silence.
- `sH = smoothstep((ls - (Hi - KneeHi/2)) / KneeHi)`, `sL` likewise around `Lo = min(Lower, Higher)` (Knee 1..24 dB), `sH = min(sH, sL)`; `Quiet = 1 - sL`, `Mid = sL - sH`, `Loud = sH`. Exact sum 1, never negative.
- Silence sleep, constant 4096 latency, Size-change mute (`4096 + N + 128` samples) as in Transient Split.

### Telemetry (`buffer~ ---loudsplitscope @samps 544`, bound on loadbang by `scope ---loudsplitscope`; the face gets `setbuffer ---loudsplitscope`)
`0..127` Mid level per column, `128..255` Quiet post, `256..383` Mid post, `384..511` Loud post (0..1 over -100..0 dB), `512` frame counter, `513` samplerate, `514` N, `515` awake, `516` input peak, `517` mean Loud mask, `518` latency, `519` output selection, `520` effective Higher Threshold (dB, tilt-domain at 1 kHz; Relative: frame max + value), `521` effective Lower Threshold, `522` loudest bin level. Written about every 1024 samples. The face polls at 15 fps, smooths the frame max for the Relative lines and only redraws when the frame counter moves.

### Rack preset
`devices/Loud Split.adg`: one Audio Effect Rack, chains `Quiet`, `Mid`, `Loud`, each holding the device with `Output = Auto` (chain index, clamped to 3; outside a rack the sum = pass-through).

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_loudsplit.py && node tests/test_loudsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Loud Split.amxd"   # preview: previews/loud_split.png
scripts/vm_spectral.sh audio loudsplit        # real Max, in the VM (optional case names; queues behind other harness runs)
```
`tests/test_loudsplit.js`: reference sum identity (< -250 dB, per-bin sum 1, non negative) at 1k / 2k / 4k and nine settings (Relative, Tilt +/-, Rise / Fall, knees 1 / 24, crossed thresholds), loud / mid / quiet partials, Relative invariance at -30 dB input, hard / smooth knees, thresholds, Tilt weights, Rise / Fall step responses in blocks (Rise 8 / 16 -> 10 / 20 blocks to Loud), silence, stereo image; ranges / defaults, fractional Rise / Fall (0.13..125 coefficients, 0.5 / 1.5 / 2 blocks to Loud), the Mix sum identity, Mix wiring and the off-face parameters; the GenExpr functions transpiled to JS (masks vs `lsMasks` to 1e-9 for six settings, sum null < -250 dB, telemetry bounds); GenExpr lint; the link module; the built device (header, latency, geometry, panels last, `---` buffer names, parameter table, wiring incl. the `active` route, `.adg`).
Audio cases (`scripts/run_loudsplit_audio_tests.py`, probe `tests/loudsplit_probe.js`, three real core instances per case: outsel 1, 2, 3; stimuli: `mix` = 500 Hz at -8 dB + 1.5 kHz at -36 dB + 3 kHz at -54 dB + faint noise, `ramp`, `steps`): `sum_1024/2048/4096`, `sum_defaults`, `sum_mix50` / `sum_mix0` (Mix 50 % / 0 %; at 0 each chain is dry / 3) and `sum_2048_rel / tilt_up / tilt_down / riskfall / knee / cross` (Quiet + Mid + Loud vs the input delayed 4096: about -149 dB, bound -90), `partials` (500 Hz 99 % Loud, 1.5 kHz 100 % Mid, 3 kHz 100 % Quiet, right = left / 2), `rel_loud` / `rel_quiet` (same split at -30 dB input; telemetry thresholds = frame max - 12 / - 36) / `abs_quiet` (the same tone at -38 dB is Mid), `thr_move`, `tilt_off` (3 kHz 99.8 % Quiet) / `tilt_up` (Tilt +6 dB/oct: 80 % Mid), `ramp` (-90 -> 0 dB sine: 100 % Quiet / Mid / Loud in the first quarter / middle / last quarter), `rise_0` / `rise_16` (Loud share in the 120 ms after a rise 0.995 vs 0.002), `fall_0` / `fall_16` (Loud share 40..160 ms after a fall 0.000 vs 0.433), `silence` (exact zeros, telemetry asleep), `latency_1024/2048/4096` (impulse out of the summed channels exactly 4096 samples later, unity) and telemetry.

### Known rough edges
- **Linking is verified only with mocks** (`tests/spectral_link_tests.js`), not yet in real Live; the Alt / Shift knee drag and the solo dot release (`ondrag` with `but = 0`) are verified only in the Node reference, not in a real v8ui. Between a parameter change and its application by the peers (about 80-160 ms) the masks of the instances differ, so the chains do not sum to exactly 1 for a few frames.
- Classification is per bin on the raw Mid amplitude (no smoothing across bins), so the Hann neighbours of a partial sit 6 dB lower and can land in the next band down (a 500 Hz sine at -8 dB: 99 % Loud, 1 % Mid). Levels are Mid levels: a hard-panned signal reads 6 dB lower. Rise / Fall act on the dB level (an onset from silence takes about `2 * Rise` blocks to cross a threshold). Relative mode's frame maximum is per hop, so a single loud bin moves every relative threshold.
- Rack with more than 3 chains: chains >= 4 resolve to Loud (Auto clamps). CPU is not measured (all work of a hop runs in the sample that completes it, one `log10` per bin; Size 4k is the worst case).

---

## 25. Architecture of `devices/Harmonic Split.amxd` + `devices/Harmonic Split.adg` (slug `harmsplit`)

Spectral Split suite member (framework: section 22). Three channels, chain order 1 Nonharmonics (grey), 2 Harmonics A (orange), 3 Harmonics B (turquoise). Use as the FIRST device of EVERY chain of an Audio Effect Rack; `Output = Auto` resolves to the chain index (clamped to 3; outside a rack = all channels summed = pass-through). The per-bin masks of the three channels sum to exactly 1 (there is no gain control), so the chains sum to the input delayed by 4096.

### Presentation Layout (`440 x 169` px, Bitwig Harmonic Split (Spectral) display)
- **One `v8ui` canvas** (`obj-viz`, `x = 6, y = 6, w = 284, h = 134`, `src/harmsplit_ui.js`, the only v8ui; hosts `spectral_link`), laid out like Bitwig's display:
  - **Top row**: `Low 80.0 Hz` (left) and `High 1.50 kHz` (right), Detection Low-cut / High-cut Frequency (19.4 Hz..19.9 kHz, defaults 80 Hz / 1.5 kHz), the resolved channel pill (`OUT 2 HARMONICS A`, ...) between them.
  - **Bottom row**: `Tilt 0.0 dB` (+-12 dB, analysis only), the `PRE | POST` toggle (UI state, hidden `live.text` `Display Post`) and the chain chip (`CH 2/3 LINKED`), `Threshold Automatic` (Detection Threshold -120..0 dB; `Automatic` at the minimum).
  - **Plot** (`x = 4..280, y = 18..116`, log frequency 30 Hz..20 kHz, 128 columns, -100..0 dB with a 6 px strip below the -100 dB line for -120..-100): PRE = Mid spectrum bars coloured by where each column's energy goes (grey Nonharmonics / orange Harmonics A / turquoise Harmonics B); POST = the three channels as areas. Over it: harmonic ticks in the A / B colours (Pattern rule, up to Max Harm, stop where they get denser than 3 px), the **root as a white dot with crosshairs** and its frequency (when the series fundamental lies below the root, a small dim `f0 32.7 Hz  h5` note under it), the harmonic ticks at the series positions counted from the root (an odd-only series skips the even positions), and the **purple interactive analysis parameters**: vertical **Low / High lines with `L` / `H` flags** (drag horizontally) and the horizontal **Detection Threshold line** (drag vertically; dragged all the way down it reads Automatic and sits on the bottom axis; while Automatic a faint dashed line shows the effective threshold, telemetry 524). The four value texts are draggable too (vertical drag, full range in 120 px, frequencies on a log scale); double-click resets (80 Hz, 1.5 kHz, 0 dB, Automatic). The plot is dimmed outside Low..High (everything when Low >= High) and the root dot / harmonic ticks are drawn only when the root lies inside the band (always, by definition; the half-octave soft edge is kept for the demo state).
  - Low-cut / High-cut / Tilt / Threshold are **automatable `live.numbox` objects that are not on the face** (`presentation 0`, `parameter_enable 1`): the canvas draws and edits them (`outlet 0 -> route lowcut highcut tilt thr dispost -> control`), so Bitwig's exact strings (`Automatic`, `1.50 kHz`) are shown.
- **Right column** (`x = 300..432`): `Harmonics` menu (`Pattern`, `1st only | Every 2nd .. Every 24th`, default Every 2nd), `NH Sens` (Nonharmonic Sensitivity, `1..100 %`, 50), `Max Harm` (Maximum Harmonics, `0..404`, 0 = Off; float, because Live's native Int stops at 255; the widget shows `0`, the Bitwig `Off` is only in the help text), `Mix` (`0..100 %`, 100). Legend (`1 NONHARMONICS`, `2 HARMONICS A`, `3 HARMONICS B`, `Latency 4096 smp`) below.
- **Under the canvas**: `Size` tab (`1k | 2k | 4k`, default 2k), `Output` menu (`Auto | Nonharm | Harm A | Harm B | All`).
- No `live.*` widget overlaps the v8ui, the two background panels are the last boxes. Short names are unique (`Pattern`, `Max Harm`, `NH Sens`, `Tilt`, `Threshold`, `Low Cut`, `High Cut`, `Mix`, `Size`, `Output`). Chain order stays Nonharmonics, Harmonics A, Harmonics B (Bitwig lists A, B, Nonharmonics; unchanged, the `.adg` is regenerated only for the new build).
- **Channel section = the rack**: Bitwig's per-channel Gain / Pan / On / Solo / FX chain are the Audio Effect Rack chain's own volume / pan / activator / solo and its devices; Pre FX / Post FX = devices before / after the rack.

### Parameters and gen messages
| Parameter | Range | Default | Gen message |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Pattern` (Harmonics Pattern, enum index 0..23 = pattern 1..24) | 1st only, Every 2nd..24th | Every 2nd | `hpat` (`expr $f1 + 1`) |
| `NH Sens` | 1..100 % | 50 % | `sensamt` (`expr ($f1 - 1.) / 99.`) |
| `Max Harm` | 0..404 (0 = Off = 404) | 0 | `hmaxp` |
| `Tilt` | -12..+12 dB | 0 | `tiltamt` (dB) |
| `Threshold` | -120..0 dB (-120 = Automatic) | Automatic | `thrdb` |
| `Low Cut` | 19.4 Hz..19.9 kHz | 80 Hz | `flocut` |
| `High Cut` | 19.4 Hz..19.9 kHz | 1.5 kHz | `fhicut` |
| `Mix` | 0..100 % | 100 % | `wetamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Size` | 1k / 2k / 4k | 2k | `fftn` (`1024 * 2^i`) |
| `Output` | Auto / Nonharm / Harm A / Harm B / All | Auto | face resolves it, `outsel 0..3` |

Linked keys (shared by every instance in the rack): all except `Output` and `Display Post`, now including `Mix` and `Pattern`.

### Mix
Each instance outputs `wetamt * wet_own + (1 - wetamt) * dry4096 * kdry` (`kdry` = 1/3 for one of the three chains, 1 for Output = All), so the rack sum is `mix * wet + (1 - mix) * dry`, dry delayed by the same 4096 samples. Caveat: the dry share passes through each chain's devices (only the wet is the channel).

### DSP (`src/harmsplit_core.genexpr`; JS reference `hsFrame()` in `src/harmsplit_config.js`, mirrored line for line)
Mask-STFT of `spectral_split_lib` (hop N/4, Mid analysis, one real mask applied to L and R). Per hop:
- **`hs_prep`**: raw dB spectrum of the Mid amplitude and the **analysis spectrum** = raw + Tilt (`Tilt * log10(f / 1 kHz)` dB: +Tilt at 10 kHz, -Tilt at 100 Hz) + the **soft detection filters** (4th-order high-pass at Low-cut and low-pass at High-cut, `-10 log10(1 + (flo / f)^4) - 10 log10(1 + (f / fhi)^4)`, -3 dB at the cutoff, stored per bin in `hsd` channel 7). The salience spectrum `hc` = analysis dB mapped 0..1 over the 45 dB below the loudest analysis bin; `level` = loudest attenuated raw bin (dBFS, a full-scale sine reads 0). New: `hsd` channel 8 = the **spectral ripple** `yd` (hc minus its mean over +-8 bins) and channel 9 = the **band weight** `bw` of every bin (`clip((att + 12) / 9, 0, 1)`: 1 down to -3 dB of cut attenuation, 0 at -12 dB). **Tilt, Low-cut and High-cut act on the analysis only**: they say where the EVIDENCE for the series is (partials inside the band) and where the ROOT must lie; harmonics of a found series are sorted wherever they are (also above High-cut).
- **Root, series, numbering (2026-10-21, user report: a C1 square (32.7 Hz, odd harmonics) at Low 156.1 Hz / High 2.90 kHz detected nothing).** The tracker estimates the **series**: the harmonic series `F * h` that explains the partials inside the band. `F` (the series fundamental, tracked in `hst` 0, telemetry 525) may lie BELOW the band (missing or filtered fundamental) and the series may be odd-only. The **root** is the **lowest partial of that series that is present (prominent) and lies inside [Low, High]** (C1 square: the 5th harmonic, 163.5 Hz). The Pattern counts from it, **`h' = h - hr + 1`** (`hr` = the root's harmonic number in the series, `hst` 21 / telemetry 526; `hr = 1` when the fundamental itself is inside the band and present, so ordinary in-band notes are unchanged): `h' = 1` is the root, Every 2nd puts `h'` odd in A, Every 8th puts `h' = 1, 9, 17, ...` in A (C1 square: series 5, 13, 21 = 163.5, 425.1, 686.7 Hz), `1st only` puts only the root in A. **Max Harm counts `h'`** too. **Series partials below the root go to Nonharmonics** (decision: the root is defined as the lowest in-band partial, so the lower ones have no number; C1 square: 32.7 and 98.1 Hz). With a missing fundamental the root is the first present partial (220 Hz saw without its fundamental: series 220, root 440 = harmonic 2). A series whose lowest present partial is above High (2.5 kHz saw, High 1.5 kHz) or whose partials are all below Low has no root: everything is Nonharmonics. Telemetry `f0` (517) is the root; 525 / 526 / 527 are the series f0, `hr` and the odd-only flag.
- **`hs_sal`** (series estimation, `src/harmsplit_core.genexpr`; reference in `hsFrame()`): candidate fundamentals on the FIXED grid `19.4 Hz * 2^(i / 64)`, `i = 0..641`, from `max(19.4, Low / 32)` up to `High * sqrt2` (and `0.45 sr`); none when `Low >= High`. Only partials inside the band are evidence, weighted by their bin's band weight (so automating a cutoff fades partials in and out). Each candidate is scored as a full series (spacing `g`) and as an odd-only series (spacing `2 g`); the **effective spacing in bins** picks the statistic:
  - **>= 4.5 bins: peak / valley comb** over 10 positions from the lowest in-band harmonic (`h0 = max(1, ceil(0.51 Low / g))`; for an in-band fundamental `h0 = 1`: the old comb). Term `max(peak(hc +-1 bin) - max(hc half a spacing below, hc half a spacing above), 0)`, weights `bw / (1 + 0.12 j)`, score `sum / (sum w + 0.8 + 0.6 clip((3 - sum w) / 2, 0, 1))` (a candidate with fewer than about 3 weighted in-band positions is weak evidence: noise gets its maximum there, the extra regulariser took the white-noise maximum back under 0.30). The odd-only comb uses the odd harmonics among the first 7 positions (`HS_ODD_H`) and puts its valley an EMPTY EVEN position away (`min(g, max(g / 2, 2.5 bins))`; for a candidate that is a sub-series of the real one the old half-spacing valley was filled by neighbouring partials, but moving it to +-g for every g made a missing-fundamental saw lock to `g = 127`: partials 2 g, 3.5 g... leak into the +-1 bin peak window).
  - **2.2 .. 4.5 bins: spectral ripple.** Partials this close are not separate peaks in a Hann spectrum (at Size 2k and 48 kHz the C1 square's partials are 2.8 bins apart; valleys between them are only 4..15 dB deep, so peak / valley terms stay under 0.2). The series shows as a ripple of period `S` in the detrended spectrum: score `0.056 * r * sqrt(sum bw)`, `r` = correlation of `yd` with `cos(2 pi f / S)` over the first 192 weighted in-band bins (cos by recurrence). **One spacing `S` serves both phases: `r > 0` = peaks at multiples of `S` (full series, `g = S`), `r < 0` = peaks half way (odd-only series of `g = S / 2`)**; the coarse grid is scored at three sub-offsets (+-0.38 %, a coherent sum over 40+ partials needs the spacing within about 0.3 %), the winner's spacing is then refined in 11 steps of 0.2 % with a parabola. Measured (Node, 25 seeds x 3 sizes): ripple score of white noise stays under 0.30, the C1 square scores 0.67..0.73 at 2k.
  - **< 2.2 bins: not scored** (a ripple that fast aliases onto longer spacings: the first version scored from 1.1 bins and locked a 220 Hz saw to `g = 23.9` at 44.1 kHz). Consequence: **a full series needs `g >= 2.2 bins`** (Size 1k: 103 Hz, 2k: 51.6 Hz, 4k: 25.8 Hz at 48 kHz), an odd-only one `g >= 1.1 bins` (2k: 25.8 Hz). The C1 square works at 2k, the **C1 sawtooth needs Size 4k** (at 2k its partials are 1.4 bins apart: a 2048-sample frame holds 1.4 periods; nothing is reported at 2k). Sweep 45 Hz..1.4 kHz at 48 kHz: Size 4k finds saw / square / triangle in every step (0 / 79 errors), 2k fails only below 55 Hz (saw), 1k below about 200 Hz.
  - The **lowest local maximum within 78 % of the best** is taken (sub-octave check, as before), refined by a parabola over the three neighbouring candidates. A sub-multiple of the true `F` scores about half (a saw's `F / 2` has every second position empty; a square's `F / 2` almost nothing), so the series is the highest `F` that explains the in-band peaks.
- **`hs_decide`**: accept when salience >= 0.36 (>= 0.198 while tracking; `HS_ACCEPT`, white noise peaks at 0.30 in single frames and never locks: a lock needs two consecutive consistent hops), level >= the **effective threshold**; hold the old series for 8 hops, 2 consistent frames for a first lock or a jump of more than 0.05 octave (a new series also resets the held root).
  - **Automatic threshold** (Threshold at -120 dB): effective threshold = running peak level of the analysis (`lm`: instant attack, release 12 dB per second) minus 40 dB, never below -100 dB. So harmonic sorting follows the signal: a quiet sustained sound is still split (the threshold falls with it), while a note that dies 40 dB below the recent peak drops out to Nonharmonics once `lm` has decayed with it; very quiet noise never locks (the salience accept does that). Slots `hst` 17 (`lm`), 18 (effective threshold, telemetry 524), 19 (initialised). An explicit threshold (> -120) is used as is.
- The odd-only flag only counts while the series fundamental itself is unresolved (`g < 4.5` bins; telemetry 527): a resolved square or 25 % pulse is searched at every harmonic (absent ones stay Nonharmonics). Without this a 25 % pulse at 220 Hz lost its even harmonics to Nonharmonics.
- **`hs_harm`**: for each harmonic `h = 1..404` of the SERIES (odd-only: odd `h` only) the spectral peak and its prominence over the mean dB half an EFFECTIVE spacing either side. Resolved series (>= 4.5 bins): local spectral peak near `h * g` (window `max(2 bins, 15 % g)` capped at 0.45 spacing, running least-squares estimate of `g` from peaks with >= 10 dB prominence), parabolic peak frequency, present at >= 10 dB. Unresolved series: nominal position `h * g` (the spacing comes from the ripple search), level = louder of the two bins around it, **present at >= 4 dB** (measured prominences of the C1 square at 2k: 5..15 dB, Hann leakage of the neighbours 1.4 bins away). `hp` channel 4 = present flag.
- **`hs_post`**: voiced amount `v` -> smoothstep((level - effective threshold) / 4 dB), attack 0.6 / release 0.35 per hop.
- **`hs_masks`**: the **root** (lowest present in-band partial, see above; held while present with a 3 dB lower bar, a lower one replaces it at once) and the weights `hw[h] = smoothstep((prominence - pmE) / wd) * (h' <= Max Harm, 0 = 404) * v` for `h >= hr` (0 below the root), `pmE = 14 - 12 s` and `wd = 6` for a resolved series; both fall for an unresolved one (`rf = clip((S - 2) / 4, 0, 1)`: `pmE = (14 - 12 s) rf`, `wd = 1 + 5 rf`; the C1 square at 2k: 1.6 dB and 2 dB at 50 %), smoothed over hops (attack 0.7, release 0.4). **No band edge**: harmonics are sorted wherever they are. **Painted lobes instead of nearest-harmonic ownership** (an odd-only series at 1.4 bins per harmonic has absent harmonics between the present ones): every weighted harmonic paints a lobe into A or B by `max`: flat to `w0 = 0.7 + 1.3 s` bins from its refined centre, raised cosine to `w1 = w0 + 0.7 + 0.5 s`, `w1` capped at `0.35` of the effective spacing (`0.5` when the spacing is under 2.8 bins, so the lobes tile without overlap); then `mask(B) = min(mask(B), 1 - mask(A))`, `mask(Nonharmonics) = 1 - A - B`: exact sum 1 (identical to the old per-bin ownership whenever the spacing is >= 5 bins).
- **Pattern rule (`hsIsA(h', P)`)**: harmonic `h'` (counted from the root) goes to A when `(h' - 1) mod P == 0`, else B. **Pattern 1 is the special case: only the root goes to A.**
- **Nonharmonic Sensitivity** widens the lobes and lowers the prominence a partial needs (14 dB at 1 %, 2 dB at 100 % for a resolved series): lower values put more signal in Nonharmonics (monotonic, tested).
- Silence sleep, Size change mute and the constant latency 4096 are as in Transient Split; the dry for Mix is a `Delay` of 4096 (`dryL / dryR`, 4097 samples), the Mix change is smoothed (0.002 per sample).

### Telemetry (`buffer~ ---harmsplitscope @samps 544`, bound on loadbang by `scope ---harmsplitscope`; the face gets `setbuffer ---harmsplitscope`)
`0..127` Mid level per column, `128..255` Nonharmonics post, `256..383` A post, `384..511` B post (0..1 over -100..0 dB), `512` frame counter, `513` samplerate, `514` N, `515` awake, `516` input peak, `517` **root** Hz (the lowest present series partial inside the band, 0 = none), `518` salience, `519` voiced amount, `520` level dB, `521` latency, `522` output selection, `523` active harmonics (weight > 0.5), `524` effective detection threshold dB (the automatic one when Threshold = -120), `525` series fundamental Hz (may be below the band, 0 = none), `526` root harmonic number `hr` in the series, `527` odd-only series flag. Written about every 1024 samples. The face polls at 15 fps.

### Live check (`scripts/test_spectral_live.js`, `scripts/vm_spectral.sh livetest harmsplit`)
Besides the shared link / audio checks, the Harmonic Split block makes four WAVs in the VM over ssh (stdlib python, `/tmp/hs_*.wav`: naive square 220 Hz mono 44.1 kHz, naive square 110 Hz on the LEFT channel only at 48 kHz, square 440 Hz at -20 dB, triangle 220 Hz), puts each in clip slot 1.. of the Harmonic Split track and requires every chain's f0 telemetry within 1 % of the true pitch within 9 s. Measured in Live 12.4.6 (gen sr 48000): 220 Hz [220.00, 220.01, 219.99], 110 Hz left-only [109.98, 110.02, 110.00], 440 Hz [439.6, 439.6, 439.8], triangle [220.00 x3]; salience 0.43..0.61 (square) and 0.48 (triangle), voiced 1.00.

### Rack preset
`devices/Harmonic Split.adg`: one Audio Effect Rack, chains `Nonharmonics`, `Harmonics A`, `Harmonics B`, each holding the device with `Output = Auto`.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_harmsplit.py && node tests/test_harmsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Harmonic Split.amxd"   # preview: previews/harmonic_split.png
scripts/vm_spectral.sh audio harmsplit        # real Max, in the VM (optional case names)
```
`tests/test_harmsplit.js`: reference sum identity (< -250 dB at 1k / 2k / 4k, masks sum to 1 and are >= 0 in every frame); 220 Hz sawtooth with the device defaults (f0 within 0.5 %, odd harmonics 1..12 >= 98.5 % in A, even in B, Nonharmonics < 1 %, A / B energy about 3), Pattern 4 / 3 / 1, Maximum Harmonics 8 and Off, **root detection** (the telemetry `f0` is the ROOT; `series` and `hr` are asserted too: **the user's report, C1 square 32.7 Hz at Low 156.1 Hz / High 2.9 kHz / Every 8th / Size 2k, 48 and 44.1 kHz: root 163.5 Hz within 1 % in every settled frame, series 32.7, `hr` 5, salience > 0.42, series harmonics 5, 13, 21 in A and 7, 9, 11, 15..25 in B with > 0.9 share, 32.7 / 98.1 Hz in Nonharmonics, sum null < -250 dB; also Size 4k, -30 / -6 dB, a naive square; C1 sawtooth at Size 4k root 163.5 (and none at 2k); 110 Hz saw with Low 300: root 330, harmonics 1, 2 in Nonharmonics, 3, 5, 7, 9 in A, 4, 6, 8 in B; Max Harm 4 and Pattern `1st only` count from the root; white noise in that band never locks (3 seeds x 3 sizes); defaults unchanged for 220 Hz saw / square; a 2.5 kHz saw with High 1.5 kHz has no root**; the transpiled GenExpr equals the reference on the C1 square at 2k and the C1 saw / square at 4k), **the band decides where the evidence is and where the root lies** (the old assertions, now on the root) (bands 150..400 Hz, High 2 kHz, full range: 220 Hz found and harmonics 1, 2, 3, 8, 11, 20 sorted as with the defaults; 110 Hz saw with Low 300 Hz: root 330 (its 3rd harmonic, series 110); 220 Hz saw with High 100 Hz: f0 0; 2.5 kHz saw tracked with High 19.9 kHz, not with the default 1.5 kHz; Low >= High: no f0, all Nonharmonics; 60 Hz tracked with Low 19.4 Hz, not with Low 300 Hz; the detection level is measured through the filters; the GenExpr functions equal the reference also for narrow, empty and full bands), Detection Threshold explicit (-10, -60) and **Automatic** (a -70 dB sawtooth still split, very quiet noise never tracked, a note decaying 60 dB drops out), missing fundamental (series 220, root 440, `hr` 2), f0 60 / 110 / 440 / 1000 Hz, white noise (all Nonharmonics, the tracker never locks: 4 seeds x 3 sizes), **square / triangle / 25 % pulse** (square 110..880 Hz at 44.1 and 48 kHz, band-limited and naive, -6 / -30 dB: f0 within 1 %, salience > 0.42; triangle 110 / 220 Hz; pulse 110..880 Hz; full range up to 1.76 kHz; 55 Hz at Size 4k with Low 30 Hz; the band still limits a square; white noise highest salience 0.295 asserted < 0.31 over 6 seeds x 3 sizes (0.34 in the user's band; over 25 seeds x 2 s x 3 sizes x 4 bands single frames reach 0.30..0.42 in rare hops, as the old full range already did, but no lock), never locks; 220 Hz square through the split: Pattern 2 all odd harmonics in A, Pattern 4 harmonics 1, 5, 9 in A and 3, 7, 11 in B, sum null < -250 dB; the GenExpr equals the reference on a square), saw + noise (Max Harm 40: f0 stable and voiced, number of active harmonics stable, noise between the partials > 90 % in Nonharmonics, about 83 % of all the noise energy), Sensitivity monotonic, glide 150 -> 424 Hz (worst error < 0.1 %), stereo image, silence, Mix rack-sum formula, display strings (`Automatic`, `1.50 kHz`, ordinals); the GenExpr functions transpiled to JS (masks and tracker state, now including the running level / effective threshold, equal to the reference to 1e-9 for four parameter sets, sum null < -250 dB, telemetry bounds); GenExpr lint (incl. the Mix line and the series-estimation constants against the core literals); the link module; the built device (header, latency, 169 px, geometry, panels last, `---` buffer names, parameter table versus `HS_PARAMS`, ranges / defaults of every parameter, hidden automatable Low / High / Tilt / Threshold, wiring, `.adg`).
Audio cases (`scripts/run_harmsplit_audio_tests.py`, probe `tests/harmsplit_probe.js`, four real core instances per case: outsel 1, 2, 3, 0; stimulus: band-limited additive 220 Hz sawtooth (amplitude 0.25) with the right channel half the left; Goertzel per harmonic and half way between harmonics; device defaults Low 80 Hz / High 1.5 kHz / Threshold Automatic / Max Harm Off / Mix 100 %): `sum_1024/2048/4096_saw`, `sum_2048_sawnoise`, `sum_2048_pat4` (the three channels vs the input delayed 4096: about -149 dB, bound -90; `sum_2048_noise` is exact), `saw_p2` / `saw_p4` / `saw_p1` (harmonics 1..12 100.00 % in their channel, Nonharmonics 0.27 %, f0 220.00 Hz, 64 active harmonics, A / B energy 3.03 / 1.91 / 1.59, effective threshold = level - 40 dB), `saw_maxh8`, `sq_p2` / `sq_p4` / `sq_quiet` / `sq_f110` / `sq_f440` / `sq_f880` / `sq_wide_1760` (band-limited square: f0 within 1 %, salience 0.45..0.63, Nonharmonics < 0.5 %; Pattern 2 puts 99.8 % in A and 0 in B, Pattern 4 puts harmonics 1, 5, 9 in A and 3, 7, 11 in B), `tri_220` / `tri_110` (salience 0.48 / 0.39), `pulse_f220` / `pulse_f440`, `saw_cut` (band 150..400 Hz: harmonics 1..12 still 100 % sorted, f0 220.00), `band_low` (500..1500 Hz: f0 660, 220 Hz fundamental 100 % Nonharmonics), `band_high` (19.4..100 Hz: f0 0), `band_empty` (Low = High: f0 0, 100 % Nonharmonics), `band_wide` (2.5 kHz saw, High 19.9 kHz: f0 2500.00) / `band_default_2500` (default High 1.5 kHz: f0 0), `sawnoise` (noise between the partials 99.6 % in Nonharmonics), `nofund`, `noise`, `mix_0` (A energy / dry energy 0.11111 = 1/9, sum null -148.6 dB, All = dry exactly), `mix_50` (sum null -150.0 dB, All null -356 dB), `thr_above` / `quiet` (explicit thresholds: A + B share 0), `quiet_auto` (Automatic: -70 dB sawtooth split 99.73 %, effective threshold -100 dB = floor), `stereo`, `silence`, `latency_1024/2048/4096` (impulse out exactly 4096 samples later, unity). All 46 cases pass in the VM after the root-detection change (2026-10-21). New cases measured in Max: `c1_sq_2k` root 163.52 (series 32.70, `hr` 5, odd-only, salience 0.68; A partials 5 / 13 / 21 at 1.000 / 1.000 / 0.997, B partials >= 0.998, Nonharmonics 32.7 / 98.1 Hz 1.000), `c1_sq_44k` 163.51 (0.72), `c1_saw_4k` 163.51 (0.73, even partials in B >= 0.997), `c1_noise` no root, `saw110_low300` root 330 / series 110 / `hr` 3 (all shares 1.000), `nofund` root 440 / series 220. Live (`scripts/vm_spectral.sh livetest harmsplit`, gen sr 48000, all checks pass): C1 square root [163.50, 163.50, 163.50] on the three chains, series 32.70, `hr` 5, odd-only 1, salience 0.63 / 0.68 / 0.66; the older waves unchanged (220 Hz [219.97, 220.03, 220.01], 110 Hz left-only [109.99, 109.97, 109.97], 440 Hz [439.8, 439.9, 439.6], triangle 220.00 x3).

### Known rough edges
- **Linking is verified only with mocks** (`tests/spectral_link_tests.js`), not yet in real Live; for 80-160 ms after a parameter change the instances of a rack apply different values, so the chains do not sum to exactly 1 for a few frames. Size changes mute each instance on its own schedule.
- A rack with more than 3 chains: chains >= 4 resolve to Harmonics B (Auto clamps).
- **Steeply falling spectra near the band top**: a triangle scores 0.39 at 110 Hz, 0.48 at 220 Hz and only about 0.32..0.34 at 440 / 880 Hz with the default High 1.5 kHz (its harmonics 3, 5 are weak and above High-cut attenuated), so it is not tracked there (it is with the full range open: 0.48 up to 1.76 kHz). A naive (aliased) 880 Hz saw at 48 kHz is also not tracked (salience 0.35, octave error with the full range). The odd comb is limited to harmonics 1, 3, 5, 7 on purpose (see `hs_sal`).
- Very loud noise over a quiet harmonic signal (saw 0.1 + noise 0.3, about -10 dB SNR) is not tracked any more (salience about 0.23 < 0.36); the 0.36 accept is the price for never locking in noise.
- **Automatic threshold is a design choice, not Bitwig's** (their algorithm is not public): running peak level - 40 dB (12 dB/s release, floor -100 dB). It cannot gate on an absolute noise floor, so a steady loud noise bed above a quiet harmonic sound is handled by the salience accept, not by the threshold.
- `Max Harm` shows `0` in the widget (Live's unit format cannot print `Off`) and `Threshold` / `Low Cut` / `High Cut` / `Tilt` are only on the display (hidden automatable parameters).
- Maximum Harmonics up to 404 and the removal of the band edge mean harmonics are now sorted up to the Nyquist limit; prominence tests keep noise out, but weak upper partials near the noise level flicker in and out (active-harmonic count not stable above about harmonic 60).
- The tracker is monophonic: chords or several voices give a weak, flickering or sub-octave f0 (hysteresis and the 2-frame jump rule keep it from flickering, but it can lock to the dominant voice). A **pure sine** has no harmonic series (salience about 0.1, below the 0.36 accept), so it is not tracked and goes to Nonharmonics. Very low fundamentals need Size 4k (60 Hz at 1k is 1.3 bins per harmonic: lobes are capped at 0.35 of the spacing and the prominence test rejects most of them).
- **Resolution limits of the series estimate** (see `hs_sal`): a full series needs `g >= 2.2 bins` (51.6 Hz at Size 2k, 25.8 Hz at 4k, 103 Hz at 1k; 48 kHz), an odd-only one `g >= 1.1 bins`; below that nothing is reported (no wrong root; the first version aliased). Sorting an unresolved series is approximate: partials 2.8 bins apart overlap in the Hann main lobes, so the A / B shares at the partials are 0.9+ (C1 square at 2k), not 0.985+ like a resolved series. At Size 1k a 220 Hz sawtooth still tracks and splits cleanly (4.7 bins per harmonic), but harmonics closer than about 3 bins cannot be separated. Size 4k resolves everything from 26 Hz up (sweep 45 Hz..1.4 kHz: no error); its latency is the same constant 4096, but its 85 ms frame follows fast pitch changes more slowly.
- **Root numbering shifts with the band.** The pattern counts from the lowest present in-band partial, so moving Low-cut across a partial (or a fundamental sitting just below Low, e.g. 70 Hz with Low 80 Hz: root 140 Hz = harmonic 2) renumbers A / B. Before 2026-10-21 a fundamental just outside the edge was still tracked (soft half-octave edge); now the edge is hard for the root, soft only for the evidence. Octave errors of the series (F / 2 chosen) keep the root frequency but not the alternation (full-series pattern on a half-frequency series: B stays empty).
- Inter-harmonic noise inside a partial's lobe (about +-1.4 bins at 50 % sensitivity) goes to the harmonic channels with the partial: with white noise about 78 % of the noise energy stays in Nonharmonics, not 100 %.
- **CPU**: all the work of a hop (two FFTs, 2049 bins of analysis, the series search, 404 harmonic probes, the lobe painting, 4 x 128 telemetry columns) runs in the sample that completes the hop (no time slicing). The series search is about 640 candidates x 10 positions (peak / valley comb) plus the ripple correlation (about 66 spacings x 3 sub-offsets x 192 bins, the same at every Size) plus about 12 more correlations for the winner. Node (V8) reference, whole `hsFrame` per hop: 0.15..0.25 ms with the new estimator versus 0.05..0.13 ms before (hop = 5.3 / 10.7 / 21.3 ms at 1k / 2k / 4k, under 5 % of a hop). Real gen in Max (`scripts/measure_harmsplit_cpu.py`, NonRealTime wall seconds per second of audio, 4 cores + stimulus, VM): old core 0.39 / 0.17 / 0.18 (1k / 2k / 4k), new core 0.23 / 0.21 / 0.19, i.e. the difference is inside the launch-time noise of the method (no measurable cost, no time slicing needed). Gen lesson: a user function with a loop (`hs_cosr`) called from inside loops made the whole gen fail to compile silently (all outputs 0): the ripple correlation is inlined in `hs_sal`. No `audio_device` perf case.
- GenExpr lessons (see the `spectral_split_lib` header): no user function call in an `if` condition or in `poke()` arguments, single `return` at function level, variables first assigned inside an `if` are initialised before it.

---

## 26. Architecture of `devices/Freq Split.amxd` + `devices/Freq Split.adg` (slug `freqsplit`)

Bitwig Freq Split (Spectral) copy: a spectral SORTER that "divides the signal into frequency groups and sorts them into one of four channels" (1 red, 2 blue, 3 yellow, 4 magenta). Not a crossover: up to 1024 narrow splits rotate through the four channels. Use it as the FIRST device of EVERY chain of an Audio Effect Rack (the `.adg` does exactly that); every instance computes the identical pattern and per-bin masks from the linked parameters and outputs only its own channel (`Output` = Auto: the chain index, clamped to 4; outside a rack = all summed). The four per-bin masks sum to 1, so the chains sum back to the input delayed by 4096. There is no signal analysis: masks depend only on the parameters, the FFT size and the sample rate.

### Bitwig -> Live mapping
- **Channel Section** (per-channel Gain `-inf..+18.1 dB`, Pan, On, Solo, per-channel FX chain, level meters) = the Audio Effect Rack's own chain volume / pan / Chain Activator / Solo and the devices after Freq Split in each chain. Not duplicated in the device. **Pre FX / Post FX chains** = devices before / after the rack. Because the rack's chain Volume sits after the device (and the Spectral Limiter is inside the device), Bitwig's note "each channel's Gain is applied before the limiter" does not hold: here the limiter acts before the chain gain.
- **Spectrum Display** (inspector Pre / Post) = the `Display` tab on the face (`Pre` default: the analysis spectrum, tinted by each column's channel mix; `Post`: the four channel outputs after masks and limiter). The display colours every frequency area with its channel colour(s) (stacked masks, crossfades blend).

### Parameters (all real automatable `live.*`; short names are unique)
| Short name | Range | Default | Gen message |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Splits` (Frequency Splits) | `1.00 .. 1024.00`, continuous, exponent 3 | `16.00` | `nsplit` |
| `Insert` (Split Insertion Direction) | `High` (arrow <-, at the right) / `Mid` (<->) / `Low` (->, at the left) | `High` | `sdir` 0 / 1 / 2 |
| `XFade` (Crossfade Amount) | `0 .. 100 %` | `0 %` | `xfadeamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Nudge` (Split Nudge) | `-4.00 .. +4.00`, wrapping, fractional crossfades | `0.00` | `nudgeamt` |
| `Spin` (Split Spin) | `-50.0 .. +50.0 %`, wrapping | `0 %` | `spinamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Bend` / `Pinch` | `-100 .. +100 %` each | `0 %` | `bendamt` / `pinchamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Limiter` (Spectral Limiter) / `Lim Thr` | Off / On; `-120 .. +12 dB` | Off / `0.0 dB` | `limon` / `limdb` |
| `Mix` | `0 .. 100 %` | `100 %` | `mixamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Size` | `1k / 2k / 4k` | **`4k`** | `fftn` (`1024 * 2^i`) |
| `Output` | `Auto / Ch 1..4 / All` | `Auto` | face resolves it, `outsel 0..4` |
| `Display` | `Pre / Post` | `Pre` | face only |
Linked between all instances of a rack: everything except `Output` and `Display` (`Mix` included: the rack-sum identity needs every instance on the same Mix). **Default Size is 4k** (was 2k): with up to 1024 splits on a log axis starting at 20 Hz a 2k bin (23 Hz) leaves the lowest regions below one bin; 4k (11.7 Hz) halves that. The latency stays 4096 at every size, only CPU grows (not measured).

### Frequency axis and pattern (`src/freqsplit_config.js` `fsPattern` / `fsMaskAt`; GenExpr `fs_pattern` / `fs_masks`)
- **Axis**: `x = 0..1` over log frequency 20 Hz .. 20 kHz (`f = 20 Hz * 1000^x`, sample-rate independent). Bins below 20 Hz / above 20 kHz clamp to the edge regions. Masks are evaluated at the **bin centres**, so regions narrower than a bin alias (modulation joy, intended); 1024 splits over 3 decades are about 0.0029 decade wide, i.e. 1 bin at about 1.7 kHz at 4k.
- **Splits n = K + f** (`K = floor(n)`, fraction `f`): the pattern has `F = K + 1` full regions of weight 1 plus one PARTIAL region of weight `f` inserted at the insertion point; the regions always span the whole axis (normalised by `T = F + f = n + 1`), so the others shrink as the new one grows. Slots in axis order: High `[1 x F, f]`, Mid `[1 x Lc, f, 1 x (F - Lc)]` (`Lc = ceil(F / 2)`), Low `[f, 1 x F]`. So a modulated `Splits` makes splits emerge from the right, the middle or the left. n = 1 gives 2 regions, n = 16 gives 17.
- **Channels**: slot base High `s`, Low `s + 3 - F` (top region = channel 4), Mid lower part from the bottom / upper part from the top; stable when the count grows (a partial region that becomes full recolours nothing). `ch = (base + floor(Nudge mod 4)) mod 4`; the fractional part `phi` crossfades the assignment: `mask_c = (1 - phi) m[c - a] + phi m[c - a - 1]` (still sums to 1). `Nudge +2` moves channel 1 -> 3 and 4 -> 2; +-4 wrap to 0.
- **Bin evaluation** (per bin, 2 `pow` + 1 `cos`): axis `x` -> inverse Bend / Pinch warp -> `q = wrap(p - Spin)` -> `t = q * T` -> slot and raised-cosine crossfade with its neighbour -> channel masks. Bend: midpoint `m = 0.5 + 0.4 Bend` (the dot's x), `u -> u^gamma`, `gamma = ln m / ln 0.5` (negative packs low, positive high). Pinch around `m`: `q = 3^Pinch`, the per-side normalised distance to `m` is raised to `q` (positive bunches toward the middle, negative toward the edges). Spin shifts the pattern by `Spin` (fraction of the axis) with wrap; the seam of the wrapped pattern is a hard edge.
- **Crossfade X**: at the boundary of two slots a raised-cosine transition of half width `X * 0.5 * min(width of the two slots)` (pattern units), so neighbouring transitions never overlap. Caveat: a split growing in at the middle (Mid) starts as a hard edge (the new thin region narrows the crossfade at that boundary).
- **Masks are cached**: refilled in a hop only when one of the controls, N or the sample rate changed (`chg` in the core). `fs_limit` and the Mix are as before / below.
- **Spectral Limiter** (`fs_limit`, only while on): per bin `g = min(1, thr / (mask * amp))`, `amp` = max(|L|,|R|); the channel mask is applied first (Bitwig: Gain before limiter; here the rack's chain gain comes after). A loud sine is capped to about 1.3 x the threshold (a Hann-windowed sine spreads over 3 bins). It breaks the sum identity (explicit gain); Output All limits the sum.
- **Mix identity in a rack**: `out = mix * wet_own + (1 - mix) * dry / K`, dry delayed by the same 4096 samples (`Delay dryL(4097)`), `K = 4` while the instance outputs one channel (Auto in a rack, `Ch n`), `K = 1` for Output All / outside a rack. The rack sums four chains: `mix * input + (1 - mix) * input`, identity for every Mix. Caveat: the dry share passes through each chain's devices (FX in a chain also process dry / 4; a chain's volume / mute changes the dry level too); a rack with fewer than four chains no longer sums to the input. `Mix` is slewed (one-pole 0.002 / sample, same in every instance). Output All is a normal dry/wet.

### Presentation Layout (`480 x 169` px, Bitwig's display layout)
- **Top row (`y = 6`)**: `Splits` numbox (`x = 42..98`), header v8ui `obj-viz-top` (`x = 104..264, 160 x 15`, `src/freqsplit_top_ui.js`: the three direction arrows `<- <-> ->`, click = set `Insert`, selected one orange; and the crossfade ramp glyph), `XFade` numbox (`x = 268..334`). Live's UI font has no arrow glyphs, so the arrows are drawn by the v8ui and drive the real `Insert` live.tab, which is kept OFF the face (`presentation 0`, still automatable / linked); it echoes back as `set_dir`.
- **Display** (`obj-viz`, v8ui, `x = 6, y = 23, w = 330, h = 119`, `src/freqsplit_ui.js`): HUD (`OUT 2 BLUE` pill, `CH 2/4 LINKED`, `PRE`/`POST`), log-frequency plot (stacked channel-colour mask columns, channel numbers in regions wider than 13 px, split lines up to 120 splits), the white XY dot (x = Bend = centre frequency, y = Pinch; drag, double-click resets both), orange limiter line + dB pill (drag vertically, double-click 0 dB; pill only while the limiter is on or dragged), dragging anywhere else in the plot = Spin (the pattern follows the mouse and wraps; double-click resets).
- **Bottom row (`y = 146`)**: `Nudge`, `Spin`, `Bend`, `Pinch` numboxes with labels.
- **Right column (`x = 344..474`)**: `Display` tab (Pre | Post), `Limiter` toggle + `Lim Thr` numbox, `Mix` dial, `Size` tab, `Output` menu, latency note. Two background panels last; no `live.*` overlaps a v8ui; two v8uis in all.
- `scripts/render_preview.py` does not apply `parameter_units` strings (the preview shows `16.0` / `0.0` where Live shows `16.00` / `0.00 %`) and draws every numbox as a plain bar; the preview display state is the control defaults (16 splits, High, 0 %, Pre, Output Auto -> chain 2).

### Telemetry
`buffer~ ---freqsplitscope @samps 704` (bound on loadbang by `scope ---freqsplitscope`; the face gets `setbuffer ---freqsplitscope`): `0..127` Mid level per column, `128..639` the four channels after masks / limiter (4 x 128, 0..1 over -100..0 dB), `640` frame counter, `641` samplerate, `642` N, `643` awake, `644` input peak, `645` latency, `646` output selection, `647` limiter on. About every 1024 samples; the face polls at 15 fps. Split positions are not in the telemetry (the face computes them with the same `fsPattern`).

### Rack preset
`devices/Freq Split.adg`: one Audio Effect Rack, chains `Red`, `Blue`, `Yellow`, `Magenta`, each holding the device with `Output = Auto`.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_freqsplit.py && node tests/test_freqsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Freq Split.amxd"   # preview: previews/freq_split.png
scripts/vm_spectral.sh audio freqsplit        # real Max, in the VM (optional case names)
```
Two typecheck configs: `tsconfig.freqsplit.json` (config + link + canvas) and `tsconfig.freqsplit-top.json` (header strip).
`tests/test_freqsplit.js`: pattern invariants over 36000 parameter sets (every direction, counts 1..1024 incl. fractions, Bend / Pinch / Spin / Crossfade / Nudge: split positions sorted and on the axis, region count = splits + 1, masks sum to 1, in 0..1); warp monotonic with an exact inverse; default 17-region rotation; Nudge integer / fractional (linear crossfade) / wrap at +-4; Spin slides the pattern (equals the unspun pattern shifted), wraps, +-50 % identical; Bend / Pinch directions; Crossfade 0 = hard, 100 % wide; **fractional splits grow in at the top / middle / bottom, keep their channel and recolour nothing when they become full**; bin masks sum to 1 for 3 sizes x 3 rates x counts up to 1024; the 4-chain Mix identity; the Python mask port used by the audio runner equals the JS reference; the GenExpr functions transpiled to JS (masks vs reference, four-channel sum null, sines in the predicted channel, fractional Nudge shares evenly, limiter cap / pass, silence, telemetry bounds); GenExpr lint; the link module; the built device (header, latency, 169 px, geometry, no live.* over a v8ui, panels last, `---` buffer names, parameter table incl. units, wiring, `.adg`).
Audio cases (`scripts/run_freqsplit_audio_tests.py`, probe `tests/freqsplit_probe.js`, five real core instances per case: outsel 1..4 and 0; the Python port predicts where sines land; `--dump-masks` is the Node cross-check hook): `sum_*` (default / 1k / 2k, 1 split, 3.5, 1024 at Crossfade 50 %, 1024 Mid shaped, 7.5 Low with Nudge 2.5, 32 Mid Crossfade 100 %, 100.3 with Bend / Pinch / Spin / Nudge; the four channels vs the input delayed 4096, bound -90 dB), `iso_*` (sines at region centres, isolation > 40 dB: Nudge 0 / 1 / 2, Mid, Low, Spin, fractional count 16.5, a shaped layout), `nudge_half`, `iso_change` (nsplit and Nudge changed mid-run: catches a stale mask cache), `lim_on` / `lim_off`, `mix_30` / `mix_0` (rack sum identity; at Mix 0 each chain carries dry / 4, Output All the dry), `silence`, `latency_1k/2k/4k` (exactly 4096), `telemetry`. Measured in the VM (all 30 cases pass): every `sum_*` null -149.0 .. -150.2 dB (1024 splits, fractional counts / Nudge, Size 1k / 2k / 4k included), isolation 87.5 dB (a 112 Hz sine, 9 splits Low) .. 201 dB, 4096-sample latency exact at every size, `nudge_half` 0.0500 + 0.0500, limiter 0.8 sine -> 0.133 at -20 dB (limit 0.1) and the quiet sine 0.0300 unchanged, `mix_30` null -150.7 dB, `mix_0` chains at exactly 1/16 of the dry energy and Output All an exact identity.

### Known rough edges
- **Linking is verified only with mocks** (`tests/spectral_link_tests.js`), not in real Live by this device's own test. Between a parameter change and its application by the peers (about 80-160 ms) the masks of the instances differ, so the chains do not sum to exactly 1 for a few frames; Size changes mute each instance on its own schedule. `Mix` is linked, so a rack with unlinked instances breaks the dry identity.
- The shared Live test (`scripts/test_spectral_live.js`) was written for the old parameter set: `Spin` now spans +-50 % (a value of 30 is fine), the new parameters (`Mix`, `Display`, `Insert`) are not covered.
- Low-frequency resolution is limited by the bins (4k: 11.7 Hz). Regions narrower than a bin alias; a sine within about 3 bins of an edge is shared between two channels (Hann main lobe). With 16 splits the isolation of sines at region centres is 50+ dB.
- Mid insertion: the rotation has a seam in the middle (two neighbouring regions can share a channel when `F` is odd), and a split inserted exactly on a crossfaded boundary starts hard (see Crossfade).
- The limiter caps a loud sine near, not exactly at, the threshold. CPU is not measured: a hop does two FFTs plus (only when a control changed) the mask fill (2049 bins x 2 `pow`) in one sample; modulating `Splits` / `Spin` refills every hop. No `audio_device` perf case.
