## 27. Architecture of `devices/Reverb+.amxd` + `devices/Reverb+ End.amxd` + `devices/Reverb+.adg` (reverb whose output runs through a Live device chain)

A sibling of Delay+ (section 15) that reuses its cross-device machinery. **Six algorithms** (Plate, Hall, Room, Chamber, Spring, Convolution with your own impulse-response file) in ONE gen core; the reverb's output goes through the devices between `[Reverb+]` and `[Reverb+ End]` and the processed result goes back into the tank (the "outer loop"). The faces are interchangeable: `base` is the reference, variants A..E are added by their own builders (see "Face variants").

### Topology
- `[Reverb+] [user FX ...] [Reverb+ End]` in one Audio Effect Rack chain (`devices/Reverb+.adg` ships it with an empty middle). The Start gets the dry signal and outputs **only the reverb wet** into the chain; the End mixes the latency-aligned dry with the processed wet (equal-power `Dry/Wet`, `Output`, `Ducking`) and writes the processed wet back to the Start (`xfer` ch2/3) as the outer-loop return.
- **Outer loop**: `Loop` x return, through a feedback clip (`Clip Mode` Soft / Hard / Limit, `Clip Level`, Delay+ semantics, applied to the return only), is added into the **tank input after the pre-delay** (before the diffusers / early reflections / convolution). Period = `Loop Time` ms, never below `B + L + 4` (`B` = vector slice from the K / B calibration, `L` = chain latency from the LOM, poked by the engine into `hdr[30]`): the return is written into the ring `lpr` when it arrives (it left `B + L` samples earlier) and read `period - B - L` samples later, so a wet sample re-enters the tank exactly `period` samples after it left. The header shows `LOOP MIN x ms` while clamped. `Loop 0` adds exactly 0 (`tests` compare the tank input peak with the input peak bit for bit). Freeze fades input and return over 10 ms.
- **Unpaired Start** = a plain reverb (Dry/Wet and Output are applied inside the Start, no ducking; the output blends over 512 samples as the End appears / goes away). Delay+ passes dry when unpaired; a standalone reverb is more useful. **End without a Start** passes its input through. The Start reports latency 0 (its wet path is internal).
- Cross-device timing, `K` / `B` calibration (counter statistics, then an audio cross-correlation in the End), the pairing by bracket matching, the `Global("reverbplus_bus")` registry, `L` from `latency_in_samples` (racks counted as their longest chain) are copies of Delay+ (`src/reverbplus_link.js`, `src/reverbplus_end.genexpr`); buffer names are `---rpxfer` (5 ch x 65536), `---rphdr` (64), `---rpscope` (1024), `---rpspec`, `---rpir`, `---rpirspec`, `---rpirview` (`---` names, not `#0-`: in a top-level device `#0` is not expanded).

### Files
`src/reverbplus_start.genexpr` (core; built as `cepstrum_lib + reverbplus_conv + start`), `src/reverbplus_conv.genexpr`, `src/reverbplus_end.genexpr`, `src/reverbplus_config.js` (constants, scope / header layouts, pure math, IR pipeline, telemetry helpers: prepended into every script and required by the tests), `src/reverbplus_link.js`, `src/reverbplus_dsp.js` (deferred gen), `src/reverbplus_engine.js` + `src/reverbplus_panels.js` (the engine), `src/reverbplus_face_common.js` (prelude of every face), `src/reverbplus_face.js` + `src/reverbplus_face_math.js` (the face), `src/reverbplus_end_ui.js`, `scripts/build_reverbplus.py`, `scripts/reverbplus_layouts/` (+ `README.md` = the layout contract), `scripts/make_reverbplus_sets.py` (wraps the Delay+ set generator), `scripts/run_reverbplus_audio_tests.py` + `tests/reverbplus_probe.js`, `scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh`, `scripts/test_reverbplus_live.js`, `tests/test_reverbplus.js`, `tests/test_reverbplus_face.js`, `tsconfig.reverbplus-{engine,end,face}.json`.

### Parameters (`P` in `scripts/build_reverbplus.py`; Live short names unique)
`Algorithm` (Plate / Hall / Room / Chamber / Spring / Convolution, default Hall), `Pre-Delay` 0..500 ms (20), `Size` 0..100 % (60), `Decay Time` 0.1..60 s (2.5; RT60 at the middle frequencies), `Diffusion` (80 %), `HF Damping` 1k..20 kHz (7k), `Bass Multiplier` 0.25..4x (1.2), `Crossover` 100..1000 Hz (350), `Mod Rate` 0.05..5 Hz, `Mod Depth` (25 %), `Early Level` / `Late Level` -70..+6 dB (-70 = off), `Low Cut` 20..1000 Hz, `High Cut` 1k..20 kHz, `Width` 0..150 %, `Freeze`, `Loop Feedback` 0..110 % (0), `Loop Time` 0..2000 ms (120), `Loop Clip Mode`, `Loop Clip Level`, `Ducking` 0..100 % (up to -24 dB on the wet while the dry is loud: level -40..0 dBFS maps onto 0..1; End side), `Duck Release`, `Dry/Wet` (35 %), `Output` -36..+12 dB, `IR Stretch` 50..200 %, `IR Start` 0..50 %, `IR Reverse`, `IR Decay` 0..100 %, and `IR File` (a `live.drop` in parameter mode, hidden). Gen messages and divisors are in the table (`prepend szp`, `dcy`, `diffp`, `dampf`, `bassm`, `xoverf`, `erlvl`, `latelvl`, `widthp`, `loopg`, `wetmix` ...: Param names that clash with gen operators are avoided). The IR controls are the engine's, not the gen's. `Low Cut` 20 Hz and `High Cut` 20 kHz (and Damping 20 kHz in Convolution) are exact bypasses of those filters.
- **Inactive parameters** (`RP_INACTIVE` in the config): the engine sets `active 0` on their `live.*` objects whatever the layout (`rpApplyActive`), and a face asks `rpActive(key)`. Convolution: Size, Decay, Diffusion, Bass Mult, Crossover, Mod Rate / Depth, Early, Freeze. Plate and Spring: Bass Mult, Crossover, Early. Everything but the IR controls applies to Hall / Room / Chamber. Convolution's Damping is a low-pass on the wet (min with High Cut); `IR Decay` is what shapes its tail.

### The algorithms (one compiled call site per big helper; only the selected one runs, the others output exact zeros)
All delay lines live in ONE `Data dl(1048576)` (a layout of power-of-two buffers is rebuilt on every algorithm entry, sized for 1.6x size and up to 96 kHz; `rp_rd` interpolates linearly, `rp_wr` writes, both functions of Data arguments). Line lengths are scaled by `0.4 + 1.2 Size` (`rpSizeScale`) and glide to a new target (one-pole 2000 samples: size sweeps bend the delays, no clicks). Per-pass gains come from `rpLineGain` (`10^(-3 len / (sr RT60))`) recomputed every 64 samples from the current lengths. Late output is level-normalised by `ns = sqrt(1 - g^2)` (clipped 0.12..1): white noise in comes out at about the same power, long decays do not get louder.
- **Plate**: Dattorro figure-8 (4 input diffusers, per branch modulated allpass -> delay -> damping -> decay allpass -> delay, cross-fed, 14 output taps), lengths at 29761 Hz scaled to the rate and by `0.75 x size`. RT60: the per-branch gain is `10^(-3 tau_branch / RT60)` (the loop crosses both branches).
- **Hall**: 16-line FDN (lengths 1031..4861 samples @48k, Hadamard mixing, `1/sqrt(n)`), stereo input through 4 Schroeder allpass diffusers per side (gain `0.7 Diffusion`), output = signed sums of the lines (two different sign patterns for L / R), line delays modulated by `Mod Depth` (6 samples at 100 %) at `Mod Rate`, 16 early-reflection taps. **Room**: 8 lines (263..919), shorter diffusers, early reflections 3..38 ms. **Chamber**: 8 lines (541..1553), dense early cluster 2..58 ms at level 1.1. All three share the one FDN code (`nl` = 16 or 8).
- **FDN line filter** (per line, in the loop): two cascaded first-order LOW SHELVES (DC gain `s = sqrt(g_low / g_mid)` each, 1 above `Crossover`; 12 dB/oct), then the mid gain `g_mid`, then `HF Damping` as a one-pole on the whole line signal. `g_low` uses `Decay x Bass Mult`. So the RT60 is the asked one in the middle band, `Bass Mult` times longer well below the crossover, shorter above the damping frequency. **Why shelves and not a complementary low / high split with a gain per band**: with different gains on the two bands, and the damping on the high band only, the loop gain exceeds 1 near the crossover (+0.5 dB at 700 Hz with Damping 2 kHz measured as a 7 s ring in a 1.5 s Chamber; an unstable tank at Decay 60 s). A first-order shelf is passive for any gain ratio (|1 + (s - 1) LP1| <= max(1, s)).
- **Early reflections** (Hall, Room, Chamber): 16 taps from one mono line: time = first + (last - first) x ((k + 0.5 + 0.35 jitter)/16)^power (deterministic jitter, alternating L / R, signs from a hash), level `exp(-t / tau)`, normalised to unit energy x the set's level; times scale with Size and glide.
- **Spring**: two loops (`D` = 32 ms x size scale and 1.37x that), each a 32-stage chain of first-order allpasses with coefficient `-(0.2 + 0.6 Diffusion)` (low frequencies delayed more: the chirp / drip) inside a feedback delay, damped by a one-pole; outputs cross-mixed (0.35). `Size` = spring length, `Diffusion` = dispersion.
- **Convolution**: below.
- **Algorithm change**: fade out 40 ms (output and tank input), clear ALL algorithm memory in 16384-sample slices per sample (about 140 samples), rebuild the line layout, fade in 40 ms. The tank input fades with the output because a running signal would otherwise hit the empty lines of the new algorithm as a step (the `switch` audio case measured a 100x click without it).
- **Freeze**: `ig` (10 ms) fades the input and the return out; the same smoothing blends every loop gain to exactly 1, the damping one-pole to a pass-through (the bass split then sums back to the input exactly) and the modulated delays to integer lengths, so the FDN / Plate / Spring loops are lossless (Hadamard is orthogonal, allpasses have unit gain). Convolution has no Freeze (inactive).
- **Safety**: the wet output is linear below 2 and soft-limited to 6 above; `fixnan`.

### Convolution (`src/reverbplus_conv.genexpr`: functions of Data arguments, run in Node by `tests/test_reverbplus.js` against direct convolution)
- **Two-stage uniform partitioning, stereo IR (mono IR -> both channels), time-sliced**: head = 128-sample blocks, FFT 256, 32 partitions = IR[0, 4096); tail = 2048-sample blocks, FFT 4096, up to 140 partitions = the rest (IR max 290816 samples = 6.06 s at 48 kHz; longer is truncated with a 4096-sample fade). One packed FFT of `L + jR` per block, unpacked into per-channel spectra for the frequency-domain delay lines (`fdh` 32 slots, `fdt` 142 slots), the multiply-accumulate result packed back into ONE inverse FFT. Jobs (load, bit reversal, one butterfly stage per job, unpack, 33 MAC chunks of 64 bins, output) run one per 4 (head) / 8 (tail) samples, so no sample runs more than one FFT pass or one MAC chunk (`rpc_pass`, the twiddle table is `fft_init` of the shared cepstrum library). The tail's output block `b = m + 2` only needs input blocks <= `m` (H = 2 x P2), which gives every job a whole block of slack.
- **Latency 256 samples (2 x 128)**, hidden inside the pre-delay: the effective pre-delay is `max(Pre-Delay, 5.3 ms)` (`rpEffectivePredelayMs`, scope `PREDELAY_MS`); the gen reads the pre-delay line `256` samples shorter.
- **IR spectra** are computed by the engine in JS (`rpIrSpectra`: head partitions of 128, tail of 2048, `[Lre, Lim, Rre, Rim]` per bin 0..N/2, float32) and written into `---rpirspec` (two banks of `BANK_FLOATS` = 1163952 floats, 9.3 MB total). `---rpirview` gets the processed IR's 512-point dB envelope and 512 min / max pairs for displays.
- **Bank swap**: the engine writes the idle bank, then the header (bank, counts, length, loaded, channels, sr mismatch) and the VERSION last; the gen fades the convolution output out over 25 ms when it sees a new version, swaps `cp` (bank / counts) at zero and fades in 25 ms (about a 50 ms crossfade, one convolution's cost). The FDL keeps the INPUT spectra, so the new IR convolves the recent past correctly.
- **IR pipeline** (`rpIrProcess`): Reverse, Start trim (% of the IR), Stretch (resample by Stretch % x device rate / file rate, Hermite; a sample-rate mismatch flags `IR RESAMPLED`), IR Decay (exponential fade reaching -60 dB at that fraction of the IR; 100 = untouched), length cap, normalisation to mean channel energy 1 (a Dirac stays a Dirac). Done in slices on a Task (`rpIrWork`: 6 ms per 8 ms tick); changing an IR control reprocesses after 200 ms from the file as loaded (kept in the engine).
- **IR file**: `IR File` is a `live.drop` in `parameter_type 4` / parameter mode (Better Sampler's handling: a live.drop only outputs in parameter mode, Live saves the path and replays it on load; `dropfile` ignores a replay of the path already loaded). The face's `Browse` goes `cmd browse_ir` -> engine -> `opendialog sound` -> `browsed` (also stored in the live.drop with `set`). `replace <path>` goes to `buffer~ ---rpir 1000 2`; its done bang sends `ir_loaded`; the engine reads it in 64k-frame chunks.

### Engine / face split and the protocol (`src/reverbplus_engine.js`)
Unlike Delay+, nothing visual owns the machinery. `obj-engine` (a v8ui NOT in presentation, 5 outlets) owns the parameter store (`rpRaw`), the link layer, the deferred gen~ (AGENTS 1d: `GEN_DSP_TEXT` / hash / `RP_GEN_PARAMS`, `/tmp/reverbplus_start_<hash>.gendsp`, hub `gate 1 1` = `rp_genin`, `rp_plugin` / `rp_plugout`, resend every 150 ms until scope `FRAMES` moves, trace in `Global("reverbplus_trace")`), the IR pipeline, the panel colours (`RP_PANEL_THEME`, `max.getcolor`) and the greying of inactive parameters. Faces are display v8uis built from `config + reverbplus_face_common.js + the layout's files` and receive `set_<key>` for the keys they subscribe to, `names`, and the state bus (`s ---rpstate` -> every face): `state dsp <ready> <error|->`, `state link <paired> <status> <latency> <partner>`, `state ir <status> <loaded> <len> <sr> <channels> <srMismatch> <name|->`. A face drives parameters with `outlet(0, "param", key, value)` (build: `route param cmd` -> `route <every key>` -> the `live.*` object; `cmd browse_ir | clear_ir` -> engine). Test hook: `receive #0-rptest` -> engine (`dropfile <path>`, `test_report` -> `Global("reverbplus_test").report_<id>`).

### Telemetry (`RP_SCOPE` in the config; tests check every slot the core writes)
Start writes every 64 samples: frame counter, sr, running algorithm, freeze, paired, `B`, `L`, `K`, MIN loop ms, effective loop ms, effective pre-delay ms, loop-clamped flag, peaks of dry in / tank in / wet out / loop return / End out L, R, `CLIP_DB`, RMS dry / wet (dB), measured RT60 (from the 512-sample block levels: time between -5 and -25 dB after an onset x 3), theoretical RT60 low / mid / high, IR flags (loaded, length, version, sample-rate mismatch), the number of active tank lines, the two ring heads, 16 tank-line energies (0..1 over -90..0 dB; Hall 16, Room / Chamber 8, Plate 4 = the four delays, Spring 16 = energy after every 4th allpass of each spring), 16 early-reflection taps (time ms, gain), the 256-entry decay ring (wet RMS dB per 512 samples), the 256-block min / max waveform ring (per 32 samples) and the algorithm state (0 normal, 1 fading out, 2 clearing, 3 fading in). **Spectra**: instead of 64 band filters in the gen the Start only pokes two 4096-sample rings (`---rpspec`: wet = what leaves the Start, dry = the input; 2 pokes per sample) and `rpSpectrum32(buf, which)` (Hann FFT 4096 + 32 log bands, 40 Hz..18 kHz) runs in the face while it is shown. The End writes `---rpscopeE` (linked, K, L, wet / dry peak, duck gain, B, out peaks).

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_reverbplus.py && node tests/test_reverbplus.js && node tests/test_reverbplus_face.js && for v in "Reverb+" "Reverb+ End"; do python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/$v.amxd"; done   # previews/reverb.png, reverb_end.png
scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh audio [case ...]    # real Max suite in the VM (shared harness, NonRealTime)
scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh run                 # Live: live + test under the Live lock (reverbplus_test.als = 3 tracks, rack [Reverb+, Limiter 6 ms, Reverb+ End])
```
`tests/test_reverbplus.js`: config math, the IR pipeline, the conv functions vs direct convolution (head only, head + tail, bank 1, one-sample tail), GenExpr lint (function order, single return, no `e<digit>`, no user call nested in `poke()`, no function-local variable named like a global Data), every `poke(scope, ..)` / `hdr` slot against `RP_SCOPE` / `RP_HDR`, **every layout** in `scripts/reverbplus_layouts` built into a temp dir (contract validation + device structure + every face v8ui run with `preview()` and `paint()` against a mock mgraphics), `tests/test_reverbplus_face.js` sub-test, the built Start and End devices.

### Measured (VM, real Max, NonRealTime at 48 kHz; `scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh audio`; Live 12.4.6 for the Live numbers)
- **RT60** (Schroeder backward integration, line through -5..-25 dB, 1 kHz band, Q 1.5; impulse into the Start alone, no filters, Damping 20 kHz): Decay 1.5 s / 3 s / Size 100 % / Size 0 % (s):
  Plate 1.407 / 3.058 / 1.521 / 1.513; Hall 1.454 / 2.974 / 1.473 / 1.428; Room 1.406 / 2.839 / 1.456 / 1.348; Chamber 1.456 / 2.803 / 1.412 / 1.371; Spring 1.528 / 3.022 / 1.474 / 1.642. Worst error 10 % (Room, Size 0), all within the 15 % gate. Plate reads 6 % short because the allpass delays count in the loop time but not as pure delay.
- **Bass Mult 2** (RT60 at 100 Hz / at 1 kHz, Crossover 350 Hz): Hall 1.79, Chamber 1.74, Room 1.60 (short lines: the shelf has fewer passes to act). **HF Damping 2 kHz**: RT60 at 6 kHz / 700 Hz: Hall 0.43, Plate 0.70, Room 0.25, Chamber 0.27, Spring 0.29.
- **Stability**: Decay 60 s with Bass Mult 4 (Hall, Chamber) or 0.25 (Plate, Room, Spring): the level falls steadily (0.8..2.7 dB per second depending on the Bass Mult), never grows.
- **Level** (energy of the impulse response of an amp-1 impulse, 0 dB = unit gain): Plate +1.6..+2.4 dB, Hall -4.3..-5.1, Room -2.9..-3.6, Chamber -1.1..-2.4, Spring -5.3..-7.4.
- **Freeze**: the level trend over 2 s (four 500 ms windows) is 0.08..0.27 dB (Plate 0.14, Hall 0.11, Room 0.13, Chamber 0.27, Spring 0.08); the window-to-window scatter of the noise-like frozen tail is 0.09..0.40 dB. Plate: while frozen the tank's delay reads (delay 1, decay allpass 2, delay 2) snap to whole samples like the modulated allpass already did (`fz` blend); fractional linear-interpolated reads inside the loop low-passed it and made the level creep (0.54 dB / 2 s before).
- **Algorithm switch** Hall -> Chamber on a 220 Hz sine: maximum second difference 0.00030 after the switch against 0.00022 steady (before the input fade it was 0.026, 100x), state back to 0, algorithm 3 running.
- **Outer loop**: wet impulses land exactly `Loop Time + 256` samples apart (Convolution on a Dirac, +-0 samples, L = 256, B = 64: 5056 apart at 100 ms), `B + L + 4 + 256` = 580 when the loop time is below the minimum (MIN flagged), ratios exactly the Loop gain; Loop 0: the tank input peak is bit-identical to the input peak with a 1.5x chain gain; Loop 110 % with a 1.5x chain gain and a Hard clip at -6 dB: tank input peak exactly 0.5012, output peak 3.9 (bounded by the output safety, finite).
- **Convolution**: null against the processed IR (noise IR 1.2 s, mono IR, reversed, Stretch 150 %): -148.8..-149.2 dB; a 6.0 s IR (288000 samples, 139 tail partitions): -148.9 dB; Stretch 150 % length ratio 1.500; mono IR: L == R exactly; a 100 ms pre-delay lands at +4800 samples exactly; Dry/Wet 75 % wet / dry +7.66 dB (equal power); an IR swapped mid-run keeps the output going.
- **CPU** (`adstatus cpu` on the real-time driver, one core, includes the harness floor of about 9 %; avg / max %): Plate 1.0 / 3, Hall 1.6 / 4, Room 1.4 / 4, Chamber 1.2 / 3, Spring 0.9 / 3, Convolution 2 s IR 2.0 / 6, **Convolution 6 s IR 3.2 / 12**.
- **In Live** (`scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh run`, 3 pairs, 103 checks pass): the GUI is up at once; the Start creates its gen~ 1.3..2.6 s after the script loads (when `live.thisdevice` fires), and **DSP is ready about 1.6..1.7 s after the gen~ was created** (gen compile; about 3.7..4.2 s after script load); K / B / L as in Delay+ (B 512 in Live vs 64 in the harness), `L == LOM sum` for Limiter lookaheads 1.5 / 3 / 6 ms, every algorithm reaches the gen and settles, Convolution loads a Core Library file as the IR through the `live.drop` path (resampled 44.1 -> 48 kHz, 250k samples, 32 + 120 partitions), IR Stretch reprocesses it, the outer loop shows MIN.


### Known rough edges
- Convolution is channel-wise stereo (IR L on input L, IR R on input R; a mono IR on both): no cross terms, so a true-stereo (four-channel) IR is not supported.
- IR resampling (Stretch, sample-rate mismatch) is Hermite without an anti-alias filter: shrinking an IR by more than 2x (Stretch 50 % of a file at twice the device rate) can alias.
- Freeze is inactive in Convolution (the engine cannot loop its own tail); the control is greyed.
- The algorithms are not level-matched to each other (about +-4 dB, see Measured); Late Level and Output trim.
- Plate RT60 reads 6 % short, Room / Chamber up to 10 % short at Size 0; the gate is 15 %.
- Line buffers are sized for 96 kHz (a higher rate clamps the delay scale to 2x, so reverbs get shorter in time); the harness runs at 48 kHz only, Live at the set's rate (only 48 kHz measured).
- Spring is a dispersive-allpass loop pair, not a physical model; its Size changes the loop length (and bends the pitch of the chirps while moving).
- Size, Pre-Delay and Algorithm changes while running glide (tape-like), they do not crossfade; only an Algorithm change fades.
- The unpaired Start mixes dry and wet itself without ducking; the End's Ducking needs the pair.
- A face cannot change the engine: a face that needs another telemetry slot must extend `RP_SCOPE` (config + core + tests), which is a shared-file edit.
- Gen compile is 1.6..1.7 s in Live (Delay+ Start 0.5..0.9 s): the gen has six algorithms; the deferred creation keeps the GUI instant. A full-set IR load takes a few seconds in the engine (a 6 s IR is 172 FFTs in 6 ms slices).
- **Measurement provenance**: the suite was run in groups while the shared VM was busy; every case passed in its last run. The CPU numbers and the loop / convolution cases were measured before the last two DSP edits (the FDN HF shelf and the line-gain refactor do not touch them; an `adstatus` re-measure was prevented by the VM, below). The Live test (103 checks) ran before the engine got its IR trace line (`reverbplus_trace`: `ir ready <file>: ...`) and the test script its `expLom` fix for `Loop Time`; both are small and were type-checked / syntax-checked only.
- **VM wedge seen at the end of this work**: after the Live runs the VM's `WindowServer` went into uninterruptible state (`U`, `ps`) and every new GUI process (Max, `screencapture`) hung in `NSApplication init` (`sample <pid>` shows `NSWorkspaceNotificationCenter`), also for other agents' harness runs; the last Live was left running at 100 % CPU and ignored SIGTERM (`kill -9` was needed). If harness cases hang with 0 CPU time, check that first; it needs a VM restart. Quit the VM's Live after a Live test when nothing else needs it.
- Not run: the user-facing drag of a file onto the `live.drop` in Live (the test hook sends `dropfile`, which is what the drop outputs), and a real Set save / reopen with a saved IR path.


### Face (`scripts/reverbplus_layouts/base.py`, "Decay Waterfall", 692 x 169 px)
Chosen from five prototypes (the others were deleted). The layout contract for any further face is `scripts/reverbplus_layouts/README.md`; `python3 scripts/build_reverbplus.py` builds this one as `devices/Reverb+.amxd`.

Analysis first: one large display of the wet spectrum over time with the decay the settings predict drawn over it, and every parameter as a dense stack of horizontal value bars. Files: `scripts/reverbplus_layouts/base.py`, `src/reverbplus_face_math.js` (pure helpers, also loaded by the tests), `src/reverbplus_face.js` (the v8ui), `tsconfig.reverbplus-face.json`, `tests/test_reverbplus_face.js`, `previews/reverb.png` (+ `reverb_conv.png`, `reverb_freeze.png`: `RC_PREVIEW=conv|freeze python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Reverb+.amxd"`, rename the output).

**Width 692**: hero 340 (the waterfall needs about 330 px for 32 log bands to read) + 3 value-bar columns of 112 px (40 px label + 69 px bar, enough for `16000 Hz` / `-70.0 dB` text) + margins. 15 px rows keep Live's snapped `live.numbox` height (15) from overlapping neighbours.

- **Hero `obj-lay-base-viz`** (`v8ui`, `x = 4, y = 4, w = 340, h = 161`, the only v8ui, outlets 2). Local rows:
  - `y = 0..15` six algorithm icon cells (26 x 15, pitch 28): plate (parallelogram), hall (arch), room (box in perspective), chamber (nested rounded boxes), spring (coil), convolution (decaying spikes). Mgraphics paths, no text; the selected cell is a cyan pill. A click sends `rpSend("algo", i)`. Status pills (`rpHeaderPills`: DSP loading / failed, no End, loop MIN, IR state) are right-aligned from `x = 172`; a pill that does not fit is dropped.
  - `y = 17..29` info row: algorithm name, `RT60 <set> meas <measured>` (telemetry `RT60_MEAS`), and, with a band selected, a pill `<freq>Hz <set RT60> / <measured band RT60>`.
  - `y = 30..121` plot: **waterfall** of 28 rows (150 ms apart, about 4.2 s) of the 32-band wet spectrum (`rpSpectrum32` of `---rpspec`, sampled in `onFrame` while the device is active), newest row in front, older rows smaller and higher (`rcRowGeom`: scale 1 -> 0.55, base `y = 112 -> 72`, ridge height 56 px at the front), painter's algorithm (each row's opaque fill hides the ones behind), coloured by level (dark -> cyan -> white -> coral, `rcRamp`, -80 dB at 0 .. -20 dB at 1). Guides at 100 Hz / 1 kHz / 10 kHz in perspective.
  - **Predicted decay** (amber): `rcRt60Curve` gives an RT60 per band (telemetry low / mid values, bass multiplier below the crossover with a 4th-order roll-off, HF damping shelf above `damp`; Convolution: telemetry low / mid / high interpolated over log frequency). From the loudest row in the history (the hit) each band falls 60 dB per RT60: drawn as amber polylines on every third row after the hit and a translucent fill on the front row, so actual ridges above / below the amber line = slower / faster than predicted. Hidden while frozen or when the hit is the newest row.
  - **Band pick**: click or drag in the plot (inverse perspective, `rcHitBand`) or in the strip selects a band; a white ribbon follows that band through every row, and the info pill shows its set RT60 and the RT60 measured from the band's own history (`rpEstimateRt60` on the 28 rows; `—` until the history holds a 25 dB fall). Clicking the same band again or a double-click clears.
  - **Freeze** (`freeze` parameter or the scope's freeze flag): the plot stops scrolling (no new rows), is tinted ice blue and a `FROZEN` pill shows in the footer.
  - **Convolution**: the processed IR's spectrogram is drawn behind the live rows as a violet surface (28 rows over the IR length, front = start of the IR, relative dB -60..0, back-end label `IR <length>`), computed ONCE per IR version from `---rpirspec`: header `IR_VER / IR_BANK / IR_NHEAD / IR_NTAIL / IR_LEN` from `---rphdr`, then each partition's 4 floats per bin (L re/im, R re/im) are turned into power per sample (`mean |X|^2 / P`, so 128- and 2048-sample partitions compare), pooled into the 32 bands (`rcIrMap`, `rcIrPsd`) and averaged per row (`rcIrAdd`, `rcIrFinish`), 8 ms per Task tick (`rcIrStep`), re-run only when the version changes. Live rows are drawn without occluder on top. Empty plot without an IR: the hint `click to load an impulse response` (click = `browse_ir`).
  - `y = 122..150` strip: dry (grey) vs wet (cyan) 32-band overlay (smoothed in dB, 0.45 / 0.55), selected band highlighted.
  - `y = 150..161` footer: `PRE <ms>`, `LOOP <ms> MIN` (telemetry `PREDELAY_MS`, `LOOP_MS`, `CLAMPED`), `FROZEN` pill, **clip LED** (round, coral, lit while `CLIP_DB < -0.1`, held 1 s then fading 0.5 s, only with Loop > 0); in Convolution the IR name and two glyph buttons (folder = `browse_ir`, x = `clear_ir`, 16 x 11 / 14 x 11 at the right edge).
  - Idle: `onFrame` returns without sampling while `rpT.active` is false (about 3 s after silence), so nothing redraws; 20 fps poll.
- **Value-bar columns** (`live.numbox appearance 2`, 69 x 15, pre-blended `activeslidercolor` (`h.blend`, 38 % accent), `textcolor` bound to `themecolor.live_lcd_control_fg`; a `live.comment` label 40 px wide at the left of each bar; group headers are bold comments). Accent per group: cyan (Space / Size / Mod), green (Tone), amber (Loop), violet (Mix). Column 1 `SPACE`: Pre-Delay, Decay, then the **swap block** `y = 59..119`, then `Freeze` (`live.text`, 110 x 15). Column 2 `TONE`: HF Damp, Bass Mult, Crossover, Low Cut, High Cut; `LOOP`: Loop, Loop Time, Clip Level, Clip Mode (`live.menu`). Column 3 `MIX`: Early, Late, Width, Dry/Wet, Output, Duck, Duck Rel; `IMPULSE FILE`: the `live.drop` (`Drop IR`).
- **Swap block** (tank-only bars replaced in Convolution): tank = `SIZE / MOD` header, Size, Diffusion, Mod Rate, Mod Depth; IR = `IMPULSE RESPONSE` header, IR Stretch, IR Start, IR Decay, IR Reverse (full-width `live.text`), at exactly the same rectangles. The hero emits `hide_tank <0|1>` / `hide_ir <0|1>` on outlet 1 whenever the algorithm side changes (`rcEmitSwap`, on `set_algo` and on any state message, so a Set saved in Convolution comes back right) -> `route hide_tank hide_ir` -> one `prepend hidden` per side -> every widget and label of that side. Saved state: tank visible, IR side `hidden 1` (so the static overlap check ignores it). The other Convolution-inapplicable bars (Decay, Bass Mult, Crossover, Early, Freeze, ...) stay in place and are greyed by the engine's `rpApplyActive`.
- **`algo` is off the face** (`presentation 0`, listed in `off_face`): still a real, automatable `live.menu`; the six icons drive it with `rpSend`, and the echo (`set_algo`) redraws them. Live's own menu is therefore not on the face.
- Panels (last, `background 1`): three column panels (`live_surface_bg`), the hero panel (`live_lcd_bg`), the body (`live_desktop`); all coloured by the engine (`panel_theme`).

**Gotchas**
- Only one v8ui: the icons, the IR buttons and the band pick all live in the hero, so no `live.*` overlaps an interactive v8ui and `tsconfig.reverbplus-face.json` can hold every script (hook names `paint` / `onclick` would otherwise clash between v8ui scripts).
- The v8ui sets `outlets = 2` after the prelude (which sets 1); the box has `numoutlets 2` via `"outs": 2`.
- `live.numbox` is snapped to 15 px in Live (measured elsewhere), so rows have a 15 px pitch with no gaps; the preview renderer draws the value text in the accent colour, so with the default colours it vanished on the bar (hence the explicit pre-blended colours).
- `render_preview.py` does not show `hidden` flips, so the IR side of the swap block is only checked by `tests/test_reverbplus_face.js` (saved flags + cords + outlet 1 messages), not by the PNG.
- The history is 28 rows x 150 ms: RT60 measurement per band needs a 25 dB fall inside 4.2 s, so very long tails show `—` (the global `RT60_MEAS` is used for the header).
- The wet spectrum comes from a 4096-sample FFT of `---rpspec` every 150 ms (2 FFTs: wet and dry), a few tenths of a millisecond in v8; 28 rows x about 33 path operations per paint at 20 fps.

**Contract requests** (none blocking)
- A way for a face to read the active algorithm's RT60 per band from the gen (today only low / mid / high telemetry + the parameters, so the per-band curve is modelled in `rcRt60Curve`).
- A face-readable per-instance "IR processed" spectrogram buffer (today the face re-derives it from the partition spectra bank, which needs the header's `IR_BANK`).
